During navigation, candidate frontiers generate multiple trajectory hypotheses that query TrajRAG for similar past trajectories, guiding large-model reasoning for waypoint selection. New experiences are continually consolidated into TrajRAG, enabling the accumulation of lifelong navigation experience. Experiments on MP3D, HM3D-v1, and HM3D-v2 show that TrajRAG effectively retrieves relevant geometric-semantic experiences and improves zero-shot ObjectNav performance.

</details>


### [73] [Exploring Entropy-based Active Learning for Fair Brain Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01706)
*Ghazal Danaee,Mélanie Gaillochet,Christian Desrosiers,Herve Lombaert,Sylvain Bouix*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种公平感知的主动学习框架，通过加权熵选择策略和掩码缩放熵，在医学图像分割中减少不同敏感属性组之间的性能差异，在合成脑MRI数据上将性能差距最多降低86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基于不确定性的主动学习方法在医学图像分割中仅关注性能指标最大化，忽视了不同敏感属性组之间的性能差异或公平性问题。尽管公平主动学习在分类任务中已有探索，但其在医学图像分割领域仍为空白。

Method: 提出公平感知主动学习框架，包含：(1) 加权熵选择策略，基于当前标记集上各组特定性能估计来调整不确定性；(2) 掩码缩放熵，限制在感兴趣区域内以解耦真实认知不确定性与解剖体积变异。在合成T1加权脑MRI上评估，使用3D U-Net分割左尾状核。

Result: 该方法在强偏置和弱偏置设置下，与标准熵相比，最终预算时分别将性能差距降低了75%和86%，并持续获得最高的公平缩放性能。

Conclusion: 本文是首批将公平主动学习引入医学图像分割的研究之一，为资源受限环境下训练更公平的模型提供了有效策略。

Abstract: Active learning (AL) has emerged as a crucial strategy for reducing the prohibitive costs associated with medical image segmentation. However, standard uncertainty-based AL methods typically focus on maximizing performance metrics, ignoring performance disparities or fairness across groups with sensitive attributes. While fair active learning has been explored in classification tasks, its intersection with medical image segmentation remains unaddressed. In this work, we introduced a fairness-aware active learning framework with a Weighted Entropy selection strategy that modulates uncertainty based on current group-specific performance estimates on the labeled set. To decouple true epistemic uncertainty from anatomical volume variances, we further utilized a masked, scaled entropy restricted to the region of interest. The framework was evaluated on synthetic T1-weighted brain MRIs with controlled left caudate bias in both strong and weak bias settings. A 3D U-Net was trained to segment the left caudate under several AL strategies, starting from both demographically balanced and strongly imbalanced initial labeled sets. Experiments demonstrated that our method markedly reduces performance disparities between groups compared to random sampling and standard uncertainty sampling. By prioritizing poorly segmented subgroups during the AL cycles, our method consistently achieved the highest equity-scaled performance and reduced the disparity metric by 75% (strong bias) and 86% (weak bias) relative to standard entropy at the final budget. Overall, this work is among the first studies on fair AL for medical image segmentation, offering an efficient strategy to train more equitable models in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [74] [Dual-branch Robust Unlearnable Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01718)
*Xianlong Wang,Hangtao Zhang,Wenbo Pan,Ziqi Zhou,Changsong Jiang,Li Zeng,Xiaohua Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DUNE——一种双分支不可学习集成扰动优化方法，分别在空间域和颜色域优化扰动，增强对防御的鲁棒性，在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上超越12种SOTA方案，平均测试准确率降低14.95%-50.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可学习样本方案因启发式设计或扰动域狭窄，对先进防御手段的鲁棒性有限，亟需一种更鲁棒的扰动优化方法。

Method: 提出DUNE双分支结构，分别在空间域和颜色域独立优化扰动，建立扰动与偏移标签的映射；进一步提出不可学习增强集成策略，在双分支优化中聚合多种预训练模型。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet数据集上，面对7种主流防御，DUNE的鲁棒性优于12种SOTA UE方案，平均测试准确率降低14.95%至50.82%。

Conclusion: DUNE通过扩展扰动域到空间和颜色双分支并集成多模型，显著提升了不可学习样本对防御的鲁棒性，有效降低了模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Unlearnable examples (UEs) aim to compromise model training by injecting imperceptible perturbations to clean samples. However, existing UE schemes exhibit limited robustness against advanced defenses due to their heuristic design or narrowly scoped domain perturbations. To address this, we propose \texttt{DUNE}, a \underline{\textbf{D}}ual-branch \underline{\textbf{UN}}learnable \underline{\textbf{E}}nsemble perturbation optimization approach. Specifically, \texttt{DUNE} separately optimizes perturbations in the spatial and color domains to establish the mapping between perturbations and shift-induced labels. This design extends the perturbation domain to increase noise intensity for improving robustness and drives the models to learn perturbation-oriented features with degraded generalization, thereby achieving unlearnability. To strengthen \texttt{DUNE}'s performance, we further propose an unlearnability-enhancing ensemble strategy that aggregates diverse pre-trained models during the dual-branch optimization. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets CIFAR-10 and ImageNet verify that \texttt{DUNE}'s robustness outperforms 12 SOTA UE schemes under 7 mainstream defenses, yielding a lower average test accuracy of 14.95\% to 50.82\%.

</details>


### [75] [SignVerse-2M: A Two-Million-Clip Pose-Native Universe of 25+ Sign Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01720)
*Sen Fang,Hongbin Zhong,Yanxin Zhang,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SignVerse-2M是一个大规模多语言姿态原生数据集，通过统一管道将原始手语视频转换为DWPose 2D姿态序列，包含约200万片段、覆盖25种以上手语，为开放世界手语识别、翻译和姿态驱动视频生成提供统一接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模手语资源仅提供原始视频-文本对齐监督且多在实验室环境制作，无法直接用于开放世界识别/翻译和现代姿态驱动手语视频生成框架：RGB模型依赖固定背景/服装，缺乏与DWPose等统一姿态表示接口兼容的数据资源。

Method: 从公开多语言手语视频资源出发，采用DWPose统一预处理管道将原始视频转换为2D姿态序列，构建约200万片段、覆盖25种以上手语的数据集，并提供数据构建管道、任务定义和SignDW Transformer基线。

Result: 构建了SignVerse-2M数据集（约200万片段，25+手语），验证了其在多语言姿态空间建模中的可行性以及与DWPose等现代姿态驱动管道的兼容性。

Conclusion: SignVerse-2M填补了手语领域缺乏姿态原生数据资源的空白，保留了真实世界视频的录制条件和说话人多样性，同时通过统一姿态表示减少了外观变化，为开放世界手语理解与生成提供了统一接口。

Abstract: Existing large-scale sign language resources typically provide supervision only at the level of raw video-text alignment and are often produced in laboratory settings. While such resources are important for semantic understanding, they do not directly provide a unified interface for open-world recognition and translation, or for modern pose-driven sign language video generation frameworks: 1. RGB-based pretrained recognition models depend heavily on fixed backgrounds or clothing conditions during recording, and are less robust in open-world settings than style-agnostic pose-processing models. 2. Recent pose-guided image/video generation models mostly use a unified keypoint representation such as DWPose as their control interface. At present, the sign language field still lacks a data resource that can directly interface with this modern pose-native paradigm while also targeting real-world open scenarios. We present SignVerse-2M, a large-scale multilingual pose-native dataset for sign language pose modeling and evaluation. Built from publicly available multilingual sign language video resources, it applies DWPose in a unified preprocessing pipeline to convert raw videos into 2D pose sequences that can be used directly for modeling, resulting in a consolidated corpus of about two million clips covering more than 25 sign languages. Unlike many laboratory datasets, this resource preserves the recording conditions and speaker diversity of real-world videos while reducing appearance variation through a unified pose representation. Toward this goal, we further provide the data construction pipeline, task definitions, and a simple SignDW Transformer baseline, demonstrating the feasibility of this resource for multilingual pose-space modeling and its compatibility with modern pose-driven pipelines, while discussing the evaluation claims it can support as well as its current limitations.

</details>


### [76] [GEASS: Training-Free Caption Steering for Hallucination Mitigation in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01733)
*Zeshang Li,Shuoyang Zhang,Jiashen Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现将自生成字幕直接嵌入VLM会导致物体幻觉反而加剧，提出GEASS模块，通过逐查询门控字幕使用量来提升VLM的接地推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在接地推理中容易出现物体幻觉，现有工作将自生成字幕视为统一正资源，但作者发现直接嵌入字幕反而会降低模型性能，因此需要更精细的字幕控制策略。

Method: 提出GEASS（Gated Evidence-Aware Selective Steering），一种无需训练的门控模块：(1) 根据干净路径的置信度门控字幕；(2) 根据字幕产生的熵减少量加权；(3) 当两个路径存在分歧时提高证据门槛。仅需额外两次前向传播。

Result: 在POPE和HallusionBench上，跨四个VLM的实验表明GEASS一致优于普通推理和对比解码方法。

Conclusion: 字幕的有用性是逐查询的而非逐语料库的属性，GEASS通过选择性门控有效缓解了字幕嵌入带来的物体幻觉问题。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) excel at grounded reasoning but remain prone to object hallucination. Recent work treats self-generated captions as a uniformly positive resource, yet we find that naively embedding one can degrade rather than help--dropping Qwen2.5-VL-3B accuracy on HallusionBench by nearly 10 points. Two structural properties explain this. First, captions anchor not only the model's final answer but also its reasoning trajectory and lexical choices. Second, caption errors are asymmetric: omissions vastly outnumber fabrications, yet each fabrication carries a much larger per-instance impact. A caption's usefulness is therefore a per-query property, not a per-corpus one. We propose GEASS (Gated Evidence-Aware Selective Steering), a training-free module that decides on each query how much of the caption the model consumes: it gates the caption by the clean path's confidence, weights it by the entropy reduction it produces, and raises the evidence bar when the two pathways disagree. Experiments on POPE and HallusionBench across four VLMs show that GEASS consistently improves over vanilla inference and contrastive decoding, with only two extra forward passes per query.

</details>


### [77] [Multi-Scale Gaussian-Language Map for Zero-shot Embodied Navigation and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01736)
*Sixian Zhang,Yiyao Wang,Xinhang Song,Keming Zhang,Zijian Xu,Shuqiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了多尺度高斯-语言地图（GLMap），通过3D高斯表示和自然语言描述的双模接口，实现显式几何与多尺度语义（实例+区域）的联合建图，在零样本下兼容大模型方法，提升目标导航与情境推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义建图方法在显式几何与多尺度语义之间存在权衡，且缺乏对大模型的原生接口，需要额外训练特征投影来实现语义对齐。

Method: 提出GLMap，包含三个关键设计：(1) 显式几何；(2) 覆盖实例和区域概念的多尺度语义；(3) 双模接口，每个语义单元同时存储自然语言描述和3D高斯表示。同时提出高斯估计器（Gaussian Estimator），从稠密点云中解析推导高斯参数，无需基于梯度的优化，实现高效增量构建。

Result: 在ObjectNav、InstNav和SQA任务上的实验表明，GLMap有效增强了目标导航和上下文推理能力，且以零样本方式兼容基于大模型的方法。

Conclusion: GLMap通过3D高斯与语言描述的双模接口，在多尺度语义建图中实现了显式几何、高效存储与渲染，并天然适配大模型，为具身导航与推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Understanding the geometric and semantic structure of environments is essential for embodied navigation and reasoning. Existing semantic mapping methods trade off between explicit geometry and multi-scale semantics, and lack a native interface for large models, thus requiring additional training of feature projection for semantic alignment. To this end, we propose the multi-scale Gaussian-Language Map (GLMap), which introduces three key designs: (1) explicit geometry, (2) multi-scale semantics covering both instance and region concepts, and (3) a dual-modality interface where each semantic unit jointly stores a natural language description and a 3D Gaussian representation. The 3D Gaussians enable compact storage and fast rendering of task-relevant images via Gaussian splatting. To enable efficient incremental construction, we further propose a Gaussian Estimator that analytically derives Gaussian parameters from dense point clouds without gradient-based optimization. Experiments on ObjectNav, InstNav, and SQA tasks show that GLMap effectively enhances target navigation and contextual reasoning, while remaining compatible with large-model-based methods in a zero-shot manner. The code is available at https://github.com/sx-zhang/GLMap.

</details>


### [78] [Adaptive Texture-aware Masking for Self-Supervised Learning in 3D Dental CBCT Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01741)
*Xinquan Yang,Jianfeng Ren,Xuguang Li,Kian Ming Lim,He Meng,Linlin Shen,Yongqiang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ATMask，一种基于纹理变化图的自适应掩码策略，用于牙科CBCT的自监督预训练，优先掩码高纹理复杂度区域以学习更好的三维表示，并发布了首个大规模CBCT预训练数据集（6,314个扫描）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MIM采用随机掩码，无法优先关注牙科CBCT中诊断关键区域（如细微病理变化和复杂解剖边界），限制了模型在医学影像上的表征学习效率。

Method: 提出ATMask策略：计算层间纹理变化图以识别高结构/纹理复杂度区域，在预训练时选择性掩码这些区域，迫使模型学习推断复杂三维形态过渡所需的上下文表示。同时构建了包含6,314个扫描的大规模CBCT数据集。

Result: 在三个下游牙科CBCT任务上，ATMask相比随机掩码和其他先进SSL基线实现了更数据高效和更强大的表示学习。

Conclusion: ATMask通过自适应掩码策略有效提升了牙科CBCT自监督预训练的效率，数据集和代码将开源。

Abstract: Cone Beam Computed Tomography (CBCT) is pivotal for 3D diagnostic imaging in dentistry. However, the development of robust AI models for volumetric analysis is often constrained by the scarcity of large, annotated datasets. Self-supervised learning (SSL), particularly Masked Image Modeling (MIM), offers a promising pathway to leverage unlabeled data. A limitation of standard MIM is its reliance on random masking, which fails to prioritize diagnostically critical regions in dental CBCT volumes, such as subtle pathological changes and intricate anatomical boundaries. To address this, we propose ATMask, a novel adaptive masking strategy. Instead of applying random masks or employing computationally intensive attention modules, ATMask computes an inter-slice texture variation map to identify regions with high structural or textural complexity. These high-variation areas are then selectively masked during pre-training, compelling the model to learn richer contextual representations essential for inferring complex 3D morphological transitions. Furthermore, we contribute the first large-scale CBCT dataset, curated from both public and private sources, comprising 6,314 scans, for the dental AI model pretraining. Extensive experiments on three downstream dental CBCT tasks demonstrate that our ATMask enables more data-efficient and powerful representation learning than standard random masking and other advanced SSL baselines. The dataset and code will be released.

</details>


### [79] [MOC-3D: Manifold-Order Consistency for Text-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01743)
*Chenyang Fan,Junshi Cheng,Wen Yang,Zihong Li,Wenfeng Zhang,Wei Hu,Yi Zhang,Pan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MOC-3D方法，通过语义视角顺序约束（基于CLIP单调性排序约束解决宏观拓扑不一致如Janus问题）和流形特征连续性模块（基于SPD流形黎曼度量促进微观纹理连续性），在ScaleDreamer框架下实现文本到3D生成的宏微观协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于SDS和2D扩散先验的文本到3D生成方法存在两方面问题：宏观拓扑不一致（如Janus问题，即多面人脸问题）和微观几何不连续。这些问题源于2D先验的视角偏差以及高CFG导致的模式寻找模糊性和梯度噪声。

Method: 基于ScaleDreamer框架，MOC-3D包含两个核心模块：1) 语义视角顺序约束模块，利用CLIP先验知识对不同视角的语义分数表示施加单调性排序约束，纠正宏观拓扑结构；2) 基于流形的特征连续性模块，在SPD流形上使用黎曼度量，通过测量黎曼空间中特征统计分布距离，促进多视角间微观纹理的平滑演化与连续性。两个模块实现宏微观协同优化。

Result: 在宏观结构一致性和微观细节连续性两方面均得到改善，有效缓解了Janus问题等拓扑不一致现象以及几何不连续问题。

Conclusion: MOC-3D通过语义视角顺序约束和流形特征连续性双模块协同，为文本到3D生成中宏微观一致性问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: With the burgeoning development of fields such as the Metaverse, Virtual Reality (VR), and Digital Twins, text-to-3D generation has emerged as a research hotspot in both academia and industry. Currently, optimization methods based on Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) utilizing 2D diffusion priors have become the mainstream technological paradigm in this field. However, due to the view bias of 2D priors and the mode-seeking ambiguity combined with gradient noise induced by high Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG), these methods still suffer from macro-topological inconsistency (e.g., the Janus problem) and micro-geometric discontinuity.
  To address these challenges, we propose MOC-3D, a text-to-3D generation method based on geometric manifold and semantic view-order consistency. Built upon the ScaleDreamer framework, our method incorporates a Semantic View-Order Constraint Module and a Manifold-based Feature Continuity Module. The former aims to rectify macro-topological inconsistency, while the latter focuses on eliminating micro-geometric discontinuity. Specifically, the Semantic View-Order Constraint Module leverages the prior knowledge of CLIP to impose a Monotonicity Rank Constraint on semantic score representations across different views, thereby providing effective guidance for the global topological structure of 3D objects. Meanwhile, the Manifold-based Feature Continuity Module employs the Riemannian Metric on the Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) manifold. By measuring the distance of feature statistical distributions in the Riemannian space, it promotes the smooth evolution and continuity of micro-textures across multi-views in a statistical sense. Under the macro-micro synergistic optimization of these two modules, our model can simultaneously improve macro-structural consistency and micro-detail continuity.

</details>


### [80] [Profile-Specific 3DMM Regression from a Single Lateral Face Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01746)
*Taiki Kanaya,Hideo Saito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对极端侧脸视图（yaw≈90°）的单图像3D人脸重建方法，通过几何条件扩散模型生成合成数据集ProfileSynth，并设计具有可见性感知下颌线正则化的侧脸特定FLAME回归基线，为基于侧脸RGB图像的非侵入式头影测量分析提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统正畸头影测量依赖侧位X光片，但频繁X光暴露存在辐射风险；现有从侧脸RGB图像检测 landmarks 的方法仅利用2D特征（如眼睛、嘴巴、耳朵、轮廓），未能充分挖掘3D面部几何（特别是面部轮廓和下颌线）的潜力；而现有3DMM回归器主要针对近正面图像开发，在极端侧脸视图下因大量面部被遮挡而性能急剧下降。因此，需要专门针对侧脸视图的3D重建方法。

Method: 1) 创建ProfileSynth数据集：通过采样FLAME形状和姿态参数（极端yaw范围），利用扩散模型生成以深度图和法线图为几何条件的逼真侧脸图像；2) 提出侧脸特定的FLAME回归基线：包含可见性感知的下颌线正则化，以处理极端侧脸下部分面部区域被遮挡的问题。

Result: 所提出的框架为"侧脸×3DMM"重建提供了实用的基线方法，为更准确、非侵入式的侧脸RGB图像头影测量分析奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 本文通过几何条件合成数据和侧脸特定回归基线，有效弥补了极端侧脸视图下3D人脸重建的空白，为临床正畸中无辐射的头影测量分析提供了有前景的技术路径。

Abstract: Single-image 3D face reconstruction is a core problem in computer vision, with important clinical applications such as cephalometric landmark analysis in orthodontics. Traditionally, this analysis relies on lateral X-ray imaging; however, frequent X-ray exposure is impractical due to radiation concerns. While recent research has explored detecting landmarks from lateral RGB images as an alternative, existing methods typically rely on 2D features such as the eyes, mouth, ears, and boundary silhouettes, failing to fully exploit the underlying 3D facial geometry spanning the facial profile and jawline, which is essential for accurate diagnosis. Meanwhile, although 3D face reconstruction from frontal views has seen significant progress, most learning-based 3D morphable model (3DMM) regressors are developed and benchmarked on near-frontal images, where appearance cues are abundant. In extreme profile views (yaw $\approx 90^\circ$), much of the face is occluded, and the available signal is dominated by boundary cues, making accurate 3D reconstruction challenging. In this paper, we bridge this gap with geometry-conditioned synthetic data and a simple profile-specific FLAME regression baseline for single lateral images. We introduce ProfileSynth, a dataset created by sampling FLAME shape and pose parameters in extreme yaw ranges and generating photorealistic profile images using a diffusion model conditioned on depth and normal maps. We further study a profile-specific baseline with visibility-aware jawline regularization. Our framework provides a practical baseline for "profile $\times$ 3DMM" reconstruction and a promising foundation for more accurate, non-invasive cephalometric analysis from lateral RGB images.

</details>


### [81] [PointCSP: Cross-Sample Semantic Propagation and Stability Preservation in Self-Supervised Point Cloud Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01759)
*Xinxing Yu,Ajian Liu,Sunyuan Qiang,Hui Ma,Liying Yang,Yuzhong Wang,Zhi Rao,Yanyan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于跨样本语义传播(CSP)的点云自监督学习框架，通过状态空间模型实现批次内样本间的语义状态传播，并引入非对称语义保持蒸馏(SPD)解决微调阶段的批次依赖问题，在多个基准数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景级点云自监督学习方法采用样本独立建模范式，难以在不同场景间保持一致的语义表征，阻碍了统一且可迁移语义空间的构建。

Method: 1) 跨样本语义传播(CSP)：将批次内样本序列化，通过状态空间模型进行语义状态传播，建模样本间动态依赖关系；2) 非对称语义保持蒸馏(SPD)：在微调阶段引入异构输入机制和语义特征对齐约束，实现语义迁移的结构对齐。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，该方法在性能和语义一致性方面均持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的CSP和SPD能有效建立跨样本语义一致性，实现全局语义对齐，并在单场景测试下保持结构化语义一致性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Scene-level point cloud self-supervised learning (PC-SSL) has demonstrated potential in enhancing the generalization capability of 3D vision models. Despite the advances in the field through existing methods, the sample-independent modeling paradigm still poses significant limitations in terms of maintaining consistent semantic representations across scenes. This challenge hinders the construction of a unified and transferable semantic space. To address this issue, we propose a PC-SSL framework based on cross-sample semantic propagation (CSP), in which samples within a batch are serialized into continuous input and processed by a state-space model to enable semantic state propagation. This mechanism explicitly models the dynamic dependencies across samples in the state space, allowing the network to establish cross-sample semantic consistency in the latent space and achieve global semantic alignment. Since serialization-based pretraining requires batch-level input organization, we further introduce an asymmetric semantic preservation distillation (SPD) during finetuning to achieve structural alignment of semantic transfer and eliminate inconsistencies caused by batch dependency. The proposed SPD ensures stable transfer of pretrained semantics through a heterogeneous input mechanism and a semantic feature alignment constraint. This enables the model to maintain structured semantic consistency and robustness under single-scene testing conditions. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both performance and semantic consistency.

</details>


### [82] [TrajShield: Trajectory-Level Safety Mediation for Defending Text-to-Video Models Against Jailbreak Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01761)
*Quanchen Zou,Nizhang Li,Wenxin Zhang,Jiaye Lin,Yangchen Zeng,Xiangzheng Zhang,Zonghao Ying*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajShield，一种无需训练、推理时防御框架，通过因果干预时间结构化语义空间，统一处理显式不安全提示、越狱攻击和时间涌现风险，在T2VSafetyBench上平均ASR降低52.44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V安全防御主要继承自文本到图像领域，仅在输入词汇层面防御，易被越狱攻击绕过；同时T2V生成存在独特的时间涌现风险，即看似安全的提示通过生成器的时间外推产生不安全内容，此前工作尚未关注这一问题。

Method: TrajShield作为训练无关的推理时防御框架，将T2V安全重新定义为对时间结构化语义空间的因果干预。通过三步流程：1）模拟提示的隐含轨迹；2）定位潜在风险的因果源头；3）执行最小侵入性改写以消除风险同时保留安全无关语义。

Result: 在T2VSafetyBench上覆盖14个安全类别和多个T2V后端，TrajShield达到最先进防御性能，平均攻击成功率降低52.44%，同时保持高语义保真度。

Conclusion: TrajShield以统一方式有效应对显式不安全提示、越狱攻击和时间涌现风险三类威胁，显著优于现有防御方法。

Abstract: Text-to-Video (T2V) models have demonstrated remarkable capability in generating temporally coherent videos from natural language prompts, yet they also risk producing unsafe content such as violence or explicit material. Existing prompt-level defenses are largely inherited from text-to-image safety and operate on the lexical surface of the input, making them vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that disguise harmful intent through rephrasing or adversarial prompting. Moreover, T2V generation introduces a distinctive challenge overlooked by prior work: temporally emergent risk, where a seemingly benign prompt leads to unsafe content through the generator's temporal extrapolation toward narrative coherence. We propose \method{}, a training-free, inference-time defense framework that reformulates T2V safety as a causal intervention in a temporally structured semantic space. TrajShield handles explicit unsafe prompts, jailbreak attacks, and temporally emergent risks in a unified manner by simulating the implied trajectory of a prompt, localizing the causal origin of potential risk, and applying a minimally invasive rewrite that neutralizes the risk while preserving safety-irrelevant semantics. Experiments on T2VSafetyBench across 14 safety categories and multiple T2V backends demonstrate that TrajShield achieves state-of-the-art defenseive performance while maintaining high semantic fidelity, substantially outperforming existing defenses, with an average ASR reduction of 52.44\%.

</details>


### [83] [MedScribe: Clinically Grounded CT Reporting through Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01779)
*Giuseppe A. Orlando,Paolo Papotti,Maria A. Zuluaga,Olivier Humbert,Marco Lorenzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedScribe是一个假设驱动框架，将放射学报告生成重构为迭代证据采集过程而非单次编码任务。它通过大语言模型动态调用病理特异性诊断工具提取局部体积特征，查询多维检索空间，在合成前积累定量证据，从而在无需微调的情况下提升CT影像报告的临床准确性、事实一致性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在3D CT报告生成中依赖全局体积嵌入压缩，导致幻觉性发现和有限的解剖学 grounding。需要一种能减少虚假陈述并增强细粒度证据支撑的可靠报告方法。

Method: 将报告生成建模为序列决策过程：LLM动态调用病理特异性诊断工具提取局部体积特征，利用这些结构化特征查询与病理特异性文本证据对齐的多维检索空间，在生成报告前显式积累定量证据。

Result: 在CT-RATE和RadChestCT数据集上，无需任务特异性微调，MedScribe在临床准确性、事实一致性和可解释性方面均优于最先进的2D和3D VLM。

Conclusion: 假设驱动的推理路径对于实现可靠的医学影像报告具有重要价值，通过结构化证据积累可有效减少幻觉并增强解剖学定位能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown potential for automated radiology report generation, yet existing approaches rely on global embedding compression of volumetric data, often leading to hallucinated findings and limited anatomical grounding in 3D CT imaging. We introduce MedScribe, a hypothesis-driven framework that reformulates report generation as an iterative evidence acquisition process rather than a single-pass encoding task. MedScribe models reporting as a sequential decision process in which a large language model dynamically invokes pathology-specific diagnostic tools to extract localized volumetric features. These structured features are used to query a multidimensional retrieval space aligned with pathology-specific textual evidence. By explicitly accumulating quantitative evidence prior to synthesis, the framework enforces fine-grained grounding and reduces unsupported claims. Without task-specific fine-tuning, MedScribe improves clinical accuracy, factual consistency, and interpretability on CT-RATE and RadChestCT compared to state-of-the-art 2D and 3D VLMs, demonstrating the value of hypothesis-driven reasoning for reliable medical image reporting.

</details>


### [84] [Embody4D: A Generalist 4D World Model for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01799)
*Peiyan Tu,Hanxin Zhu,Jingwen Sun,Shaojie Ren,Cong Wang,Jiayi Luo,Xiaoqian Cheng,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Embody4D提出了一种面向具身场景的视频到视频世界模型，能从单目视频合成任意新颖视角，通过3D感知组合合成管道解决数据稀缺、自适应噪声注入保证几何一致性、交互感知注意力机制确保操作保真度，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身世界模型大多局限于2D表示，缺乏多视角信息，难以支持具身空间推理；同时面临配对多视角数据稀缺、生成3D几何的时空一致性难以维持、操作细节易出现幻觉等挑战。

Method: (1) 3D感知组合合成管道：构建跨具身形态的机械臂与多样化背景的组合数据集，缓解数据稀缺；(2) 自适应噪声注入策略：利用图像区域置信度差异，选择性正则化扩散过程，保障时空一致性；(3) 交互感知注意力机制：显式关注机器人交互区域，确保操作保真度。

Result: 在多个实验上达到SOTA性能，能合成高保真、视角一致的视频，有效赋能下游机器人规划与学习。

Conclusion: Embody4D作为鲁棒的具身世界模型，通过解决数据、几何一致性和操作细节三大挑战，成功实现了从单目视频到任意新颖视角的高质量合成，显著提升了具身空间推理能力。

Abstract: World models have made significant progress in modeling dynamic environments; however, most embodied world models are still restricted to 2D representations, lacking the comprehensive multi-view information essential for embodied spatial reasoning. Bridging this gap is non-trivial, primarily due to challenges from severe scarcity of paired multi-view data, the difficulty of maintaining spatiotemporal consistency in generated 3D geometries, and the tendency to hallucinate manipulation details. To address these challenges, we propose Embody4D, a dedicated video-to-video world model for embodied scenarios, capable of synthesizing arbitrary novel views from a monocular video. First, to tackle data scarcity, we introduce a 3D-aware compositional synthesis pipeline to curate a heterogeneous dataset compositing cross-embodiment robotic arms with diverse backgrounds, guaranteeing broad generalization. Second, to enforce geometric stability, we devise an adaptive noise injection strategy; by leveraging confidence disparities across image regions, this method selectively regularizes the diffusion process to ensure strict spatiotemporal consistency. Finally, to guarantee manipulation fidelity, we incorporate an interaction-aware attention mechanism that explicitly attends to the robotic interaction regions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Embody4D achieves state-of-the-art performance, serving as a robust world model that synthesizes high-fidelity, view-consistent videos to empower downstream robotic planning and learning.

</details>


### [85] [Cross-Domain Adversarial Augmentation: Stabilizing GANs for Medical and Handwriting Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01815)
*Md. Sohanuzzaman Soad,Mahady Al Hady,S M Rafiuddin Rifat,Sudip Ghose*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在低资源场景下（孟加拉语手写字符和胸部X光影像）使用DCGAN进行生成式数据增强，证明生成样本能提升分类器性能，并讨论了稳定性改进、评估注意事项及隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缓解视觉任务中数据稀缺问题，探索GAN在低资源领域的生成式增强潜力。

Method: 使用DCGAN模型在64x64分辨率下训练，采用Inception Score、FID、t-SNE/UMAP评估质量，并通过分类器对比（真实数据 vs 真实+合成数据）衡量下游效用，同时进行了梯度惩罚、谱归一化等稳定性消融实验。

Result: 生成式增强提升了样本多样性，并在有限数据条件下持续提升了分类器性能。

Conclusion: 提出的协议简单可复现，为资源受限的成像任务中的生成式增强提供了强基线，同时需注意医学图像评估陷阱、数据集许可及合成数据的隐私风险。

Abstract: Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) offer a pragmatic route to mitigate data scarcity in vision tasks. We study generative augmentation across two low-resource domains: Bangla handwritten characters and chest X-ray imaging using DCGAN-style models trained at 64x64 resolution. We evaluate fidelity and diversity via Inception Score (IS), Fr'echet Inception Distance (FID), and embedding visualizations (t-SNE/UMAP), and assess downstream utility by training classifiers on real versus real synthetic data. Our experiments show that generative augmentation improves sample diversity and yields consistent gains in classifier performance under limited-data regimes. We analyze stability enhancements (e.g., gradient-penalized objectives and spectral normalization) and report ablations on synthetic-to-real ratios and sample filtering. We discuss evaluation caveats for medical images, dataset licensing, and privacy risks associated with synthetic data. The resulting protocol is simple to reproduce and provides a strong baseline for applying generative augmentation to resource-constrained imaging tasks.

</details>


### [86] [Hybrid Visual Telemetry for Bandwidth-Constrained Robotic Vision: A Pilot Study with HEVC Base Video and JPEG ROI Stills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01826)
*Natalia Trukhina,Vadim Vashkelis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向带宽受限机器人视觉系统的双通道视觉遥测方案：连续低码率视频流+选择性传输的高细节静态ROI图像，并基于x265/HEVC+JPEG的实用编解码栈建立了混合传输范式的实验方法论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有带宽受限系统依赖单一压缩视频流同时支持场景感知和机器感知，但低码率视频在保留运动和粗粒度上下文的同时，丢失了目标识别和决策所需的精细局部细节，造成不匹配问题。

Method: 提出双通道视觉遥测架构：低分辨率视频流支持动态场景理解，事件驱动的ROI高清静态图像支持近景识别。采用x265/HEVC作为基视频编码，JPEG作为ROI静态图编码，在匹配总通信预算下比较纯视频与混合方案的性能。

Result: 本研究并非证明新静态图像编码器的优越性，而是建立了混合传输范式的实验协议，包括面向无人机数据集、两种码率范围、多种ROI触发策略以及选择性传输ROI上的目标级分类精化评估。

Conclusion: 本文为后续阶段研究JPEG AI作为语义静态图像通道在相同混合架构中的应用奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: Bandwidth-constrained robotic and surveillance systems often rely on a single compressed video stream to support both continuous scene awareness and downstream machine perception. In practice, this creates a mismatch: low-bitrate video can preserve motion and coarse context, but often loses the fine local detail needed for reliable object recognition and decision-making. Motivated by a hybrid architecture in which low-resolution video supports dynamic scene understanding while eventdriven high-detail regions of interest (ROIs) support close-up identification and analytics, this paper formalizes a two-channel visual telemetry scheme in which a continuous low-bitrate video stream is augmented by selectively transmitted high-detail still ROIs. This first paper does not attempt to prove the superiority of a new still-image codec. Instead, it establishes the hybrid transmission paradigm itself using a practical and reproducible codec stack: x265/HEVC for the base video stream and JPEG stills for ROI refinement. We formulate the problem as bitrate-constrained information selection for robotic vision and define an experimental protocol in which video-only and hybrid schemes are compared under matched total communication budgets. The study is designed around UAV-oriented datasets, two practical bitrate regimes, several ROI triggering policies, and object-level classification refinement on selectively transmitted ROI stills. The resulting paper lays the methodological foundation for a second-stage investigation of JPEG AI as the semantic still-image channel within the same hybrid architecture.

</details>


### [87] [Referring Multiple Regions with Large Multimodal Models via Contextual Latent Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01827)
*Yun Xing,Hanyuan Liu,Jiahao Nie,Shijian Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CSteer，一种无需训练的方法，通过预计算上下文向量并在推理时编辑表示，引导通用多模态大模型在视觉提示下进行多区域上下文感知定位，无需微调或修改架构，在多数数据集上超越专用定位模型，达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMMs在视觉提示引导的区域级感知方面存在不足，尤其是同时涉及多个区域或需要全局上下文进行精确定位时表现不佳，而现有解决方案通常需要昂贵的微调或架构修改。

Method: CSteer是一种无需训练的方法，首先预计算隐含表示视觉定位行为的上下文向量（包括区域区分和全局上下文关注），然后在推理时通过表示编辑来引导模型进行多区域上下文感知定位。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，使用CSteer的通用LMMs在多数情况下超越了专门设计的定位LMMs，达到了新的SOTA性能。

Conclusion: CSteer为无需训练的视觉定位提供了一种有前景的解决方案，证明了通过表示编辑即可实现多区域上下文感知定位的能力。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have recently demonstrated their proficiency in holistic visual comprehension. However, most of them struggle to tackle region-level perception guided by visual prompts, especially for cases where multiple regions are referred simultaneously, or scenarios where global contexts are necessary for precise visual referring. We introduce Contextual Latent Steering (CSteer), a training-free approach for guiding general LMMs to refer multiple regions contextually, without expensive fine-tuning or architectural modifications. CSteer starts with pre-computing contextual vectors that implicitly represent visual referring behaviors, such as differentiation among regions and attention to global contexts, followed by representation editing during inference time. Experimental results on multiple datasets indicate that general LMMs with CSteer outperform tailored referring LMMs in most cases, suggesting a promising solution in training-free, and setting new state-of-the-art for this field. Code is available at https://github.com/xing0047/csteer.git.

</details>


### [88] [WindowQuant: Mixed-Precision KV Cache Quantization based on Window-Level Similarity for VLMs Inference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02262)
*Wei Tao,Xiaoyang Qu,Peiqiang Wang,Guokuan Li,Jiguang Wan,Kai Lu,Jianzong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WindowQuant，一种窗口自适应混合精度量化方法，通过窗口级量化搜索和窗口级KV缓存计算，在保持模型精度的同时显著降低视频语言模型KV缓存的内存占用和推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频语言模型（VLM）的视觉token序列过长导致推理延迟高和GPU内存消耗大，现有基于token粒度的混合精度量化方法搜索耗时且硬件计算效率低下。

Method: WindowQuant包含两个模块：1) 窗口级量化搜索：基于视觉token窗口与文本提示之间的相似度分数，快速确定KV缓存窗口的最优位宽配置；2) 窗口级KV缓存计算：在量化前对KV缓存窗口重新排序，避免混合精度量化在推理计算中的硬件低效问题。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，WindowQuant优于现有的最先进VLM模型和KV缓存量化方法。

Conclusion: WindowQuant通过窗口自适应的混合精度量化策略，有效平衡了VLM的推理效率与模型精度，解决了视觉token序列过长带来的实际部署难题。

Abstract: Recently, video language models (VLMs) have been applied in various fields. However, the visual token sequence of the VLM is too long, which may cause intolerant inference latency and GPU memory usage. Existing methods propose mixed-precision quantization to the key-value (KV) cache in VLMs based on token granularity, which is time-consuming in the search process and hardware inefficient during computation. This paper introduces a novel approach called WindowQuant, which employs window-adaptive mixed-precision quantization to optimize the KV cache. WindowQuant consists of two modules: window-level quantization search and window-level KV cache computation. Window-level quantization search quickly determines the optimal bit-width configuration of the KV cache windows based on the similarity scores between the corresponding visual token windows and the text prompt, maintaining the model accuracy. Furthermore, window-level KV cache computation reorders the KV cache windows before quantization, avoiding the hardware inefficiency caused by mixed-precision quantization in inference computation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that WindowQuant outperforms state-of-the-art VLM models and KV cache quantization methods on various datasets.

</details>


### [89] [GeoSAE: Geometric Prior-Guided Layer-Wise Sparse Autoencoder Annotation of Brain MRI Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01829)
*Favour Nerrise,Lucy Yin,Mohammad H. Abbasi,Kilian M. Pohl,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoSAE是一种几何引导的稀疏自编码器框架，通过利用基础模型学习的流形结构防止特征坍塌，并使用年龄去混杂偏相关进行特征标注。在约1.4万张T1加权MRI扫描数据上，仅用2%的嵌入维度即可预测轻度认知障碍向阿尔茨海默病的转化（AUC 0.746），跨队列可复制（r=0.97），定位区域与Braak分期一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑MRI基础模型学习丰富的解剖表征，但如何解释其编码的临床信息仍是开放问题。标准稀疏自编码器在深层Transformer层中存在严重的特征坍塌，且在阿尔茨海默病研究中，年龄几乎混杂所有临床变量，导致朴素标注不可靠。

Method: 提出GeoSAE框架，利用基础模型学习的流形结构指导SAE训练以防止特征坍塌；对每个幸存特征通过年龄去混杂的偏相关分析进行临床标注。在ADNI和AIBL两个数据集约1.4万张T1加权MRI扫描上验证。

Result: 仅用2%的嵌入维度即可预测MCI向AD转化（AUC 0.746）；共病标注特征仅达随机水平；特征跨队列再现性高（r=0.97）；定位到与Braak分期一致的神经解剖学区域。

Conclusion: 几何引导的SAE能够从冻结的脑MRI基础模型中提取可解释的生物标志物，为医学影像基础模型的可解释性提供了有效方法论。

Abstract: Brain MRI foundation models learn rich representations of anatomy, but interpreting what clinical information they encode remains an open problem. Standard sparse autoencoders (SAEs) suffer from severe feature collapse in deep transformer layers, and in Alzheimer's disease (AD) research, aging confounds nearly every clinical variable, making naive annotation unreliable. We propose GeoSAE, a geometry-guided SAE framework that uses the foundation model's learned manifold structure to prevent feature collapse and annotates each surviving feature via age-deconfounded partial correlations. Applied to ~14k T1-weighted MRI scans from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) and the Australian Imaging biomarkers and Lifestyle (AIBL) datasets, GeoSAE identifies a compact, fully interpretable feature set that predicts mild cognitive impairment (MCI)-to-AD conversion (AUC 0.746) using only 2% of the embedding dimensions, while comorbidity-annotated features achieve only chance-level performance. The identified features replicate across cohorts without retraining (r=0.97) and localize to neuroanatomically distinct regions consistent with Braak staging. This shows that geometry-guided SAEs can extract interpretable, biomarkers from frozen brain MRI foundation models.

</details>


### [90] [Disentangled Anatomy-Disease Diffusion (DADD) for Controllable Ulcerative Colitis Progression Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01848)
*Umut Dundar,Alptekin Temizel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DADD框架，通过解耦解剖结构与疾病特征，在可控疾病阶段合成纵向医学内镜图像，并利用特征净化器、三路径交叉注意力和Delta Steering机制实现高质量图像生成与类别不平衡重平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成纵向医学图像时，病理纹理与患者特异性解剖结构高度纠缠，难以在保留个体解剖特征的同时控制疾病阶段的连续变化。现有方法无法有效解耦这两种信息，限制了疾病进展模拟与下游分类任务性能。

Method: 提出DADD框架：（1）特征净化器（Feature Purifier），基于交叉注意力的擦除机制，抑制图像编码器中疾病相关通道，获得纯净解剖表示；（2）三路径交叉注意力（Triple-Pathway Cross-Attention），结合分辨率依赖路由门控，将纯净解剖token和目标疾病token注入U-Net不同层级；（3）Delta Steering，一种基于序数嵌入的训练自由方向信号，实现推理时单次前向传递的显式疾病过渡控制。

Result: 在LIMUC数据集上验证，该方法在所有严重度级别上生成高保真图像，并有效重平衡倾斜的类别分布，提升下游分类任务性能。

Conclusion: DADD成功解耦解剖结构与疾病特征，实现了可控疾病阶段的纵向医学图像合成，为溃疡性结肠炎内镜评估及类别不平衡问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Synthesizing longitudinal medical images at controllable disease stages while preserving patient-specific anatomy is hindered by the entanglement of pathological textures and structural features. We address this challenge for ulcerative colitis (UC) endoscopy, where severity follows a continuous ordinal progression along the Mayo Endoscopic Score (MES). Our framework, Disentangled Anatomy-Disease Diffusion (DADD), conditions a latent diffusion model on two complementary embeddings: a pretrained image encoder for patient anatomy and a separately trained ordinal embedder for cumulative disease severity. Since image embeddings inevitably capture disease information, we introduce a Feature Purifier, a cross-attention-based erasure mechanism that identifies and suppresses disease-correlated channels, yielding purified anatomical representations. These cleaned anatomy tokens and target disease tokens are injected into the denoising network via a Triple-Pathway Cross-Attention mechanism with resolution-dependent routing gates. This architecture leverages the U-Net hierarchy, in which different network depths encode global structure versus fine-grained pathological texture. Furthermore, we introduce Delta Steering, a training-free directional signal derived from the ordinal embeddings that enables explicit, single-pass control over disease transitions at inference without requiring additional forward passes. Validated on the LIMUC dataset, our approach produces high-fidelity images across all severity levels and effectively rebalances skewed class distributions, enhancing performance for downstream classification tasks. The dataset is available at zenodo.org/records/5827695 and the code base at github.com/umutdundar99/progressive-stable-diffusion

</details>


### [91] [DP-SfM: Dual-Pixel Structure-from-Motion without Scale Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01852)
*Lilika Makabe,Kohei Ashida,Hiroaki Santo,Fumio Okura,Yasuyuki Matsushita*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用双像素（DP）传感器拍摄的多视图图像，无需参考物体或预先标定即可自动解决多视图3D重建中的尺度模糊问题。通过DP图像中的散焦模糊与多视图重建得到的深度图相结合，估计绝对尺度，并进一步通过强度优化对齐左右DP图像。实验在多种相机和镜头下验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图3D重建（SfM+MVS）存在未知尺度模糊的问题，传统方法需要场景中存在已知尺寸的参考物体或预先标定才能消除该模糊，这限制了方法的通用性和便利性。本文旨在利用双像素传感器的特性自动解决尺度模糊问题。

Method: 1) 利用双像素（DP）传感器捕获的多视图图像中存在的散焦模糊信息；2) 结合多视图3D重建得到的尺度模糊的深度图，推导绝对尺度估计的线性方法；3) 进一步通过基于强度的优化阶段，利用交叉视图模糊核将左右DP图像相互对齐（shift back），从而精化结果。

Result: 在多种相机和镜头拍摄的不同场景中验证了所提方法的有效性，能够准确恢复绝对尺度，无需参考物体或预先标定。

Conclusion: 双像素传感器图像中的散焦模糊能够提供足够的绝对尺度信息，与多视图重建的深度图配合可自动消除尺度模糊，是一种简单、线性且有效的方法，有潜力成为多视图3D重建中解决尺度问题的实用方案。

Abstract: Multi-view 3D reconstruction, namely, structure-from-motion followed by multi-view stereo, is a fundamental component of 3D computer vision. In general, multi-view 3D reconstruction suffers from an unknown scale ambiguity unless a reference object of known size is present in the scene. In this article, we show that multi-view images captured using a dual-pixel (DP) sensor can automatically resolve the scale ambiguity, without requiring a reference object or prior calibration. Specifically, the defocus blur observed in DP images provides sufficient information to determine the absolute scale when paired with depth maps (up to scale) recovered from multi-view 3D reconstruction. Based on this observation, we develop a simple yet effective linear method to estimate the absolute scale, followed by the intensity-based optimization stage that aligns the left and right DP images by shifting them back toward each other using cross-view blur kernels. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach across diverse scenes captured with different cameras and lenses. Code and data are available at https://github.com/lilika-makabe/dp-sfm-tpami.git

</details>


### [92] [High-Fidelity Mobile Avatars with Pruned Local Blendshapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01854)
*Youyi Zhan,He Wang,Tianjia Shao,Kun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于局部线性混合形状（local linear blendshapes）的方法，从多视角视频重建高保真人体化身，无需预训练模型，通过WebGPU实现移动设备上2K分辨率120 FPS的实时渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯的人体化身方法能从多视角视频重建高质量全身模型，但依赖大量计算获取姿态相关外观，难以部署到移动设备上。虽然后续方法通过蒸馏预训练模型并使用全局姿态特征与混合形状线性组合以减少计算，实现了移动端运行，但丢失了细节。

Method: 观察到邻近高斯在身体局部区域内高度相关，可采用线性建模。因此在小身体部位使用局部线性混合形状来捕捉高斯属性的全局非线性变化。进一步提出去除属性变化较小的高斯的混合形状，得到最小化混合形状表示。该方法为端到端训练，无需预训练模型，并使用WebGPU实现多设备部署。

Result: 该方法能渲染出细节更好的高质量人体化身，在移动设备上达到2K分辨率下120 FPS的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过局部线性混合形状替代全局非线性建模，在保持移动端实时渲染能力的同时，显著提升了化身重建的细节质量。

Abstract: We propose a method to reconstruct high-fidelity human avatars from multi-view video that can run on mobile devices. Many works can model high-quality Gaussian-based full-body avatars from multi-view video. However, these methods require heavy computation to obtain pose-dependent appearance, making deployment on mobile devices very difficult. Recent methods distill from pretrained models and model pose-dependent nonlinear Gaussian attributes by linearly combining global pose features with blendshapes. Although they can run on mobile devices, they suffer some loss of detail. We observe that nearby Gaussians are often highly correlated within a local region of the body, and can be linearly modeled with less error. Therefore, we use local linear blendshapes in small body parts to capture global nonlinear changes of Gaussian attributes. To further reduce computation and model size, we propose to remove blendshapes for Gaussians whose attributes change little, yielding a minimal blendshape representation. Our method is an end-to-end training method without a pretrained model. To make it run on multiple devices, we implement our method using WebGPU. Experiments show that our method can render high-quality human avatars with better details, and can reach 120 FPS at 2K resolution on mobile devices.

</details>


### [93] [Decouple and Cache: KV Cache Construction for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01858)
*Zhanzhong Pang,Dibyadip Chatterjee,Fadime Sener,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DSCache，一种免训练的缓存构建机制，通过解耦历史缓存与即时缓存、引入位置无关编码，使预训练离线视频模型适应流式设置，在Streaming Video QA基准上获得2.5%的平均准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频VLLM在扩展到无限视频流时存在瓶颈，且现有工作多关注缓存重用策略，忽略了缓存构建机制对性能的影响。

Method: 提出解耦流式缓存(DSCache)：1)维护累积历史KV缓存的同时按需构建独立的即时缓存，解耦以保留近期输入的丰富信息；2)引入位置无关编码策略，使KV缓存支持训练时未见过的位置，防止位置溢出。

Result: 在流式视频问答基准上取得SOTA结果，相比先前方法平均准确率提升2.5%。

Conclusion: DSCache通过免训练的缓存解耦与位置无关编码，有效解决了流式视频理解中缓存构建与位置外推两大挑战。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires processing unbounded video streams with limited memory and computation, posing two key challenges. First, continuously constructing new and evicting old key-value(KV) caches is required for unbounded streams. Secondly, due to the high cost of collecting and training on unbounded streams, models must learn from short sequences while generalizing to long streams. Existing streaming VideoVLLMs fail to scale to unbounded video streams or focus on cache reuse strategies, leaving the impact of cache construction underexplored. In this paper, we propose Decoupled Streaming Cache(DSCache), a training-free cache construction mechanism that adapts pretrained offline models to streaming settings. DSCache maintains a cumulative past KV cache while constructing a separate instant cache on-demand, decoupled from past caches to preserve the informativeness of recent inputs. To enable position extrapolation beyond the training length, DSCache further incorporates a position-agnostic encoding strategy, ensuring KV caches to support unseen positions and preventing position overflow. Experiments on Streaming Video QA benchmarks demonstrate DSCache's state-of-the-art performance, with an average 2.5% accuracy gains over prior methods.

</details>


### [94] [BadmintonGRF: A Multimodal Dataset and Benchmark for Markerless Ground Reaction Force Estimation in Badminton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01876)
*Kuoye Niu,Jianwei Li,Shengze Cai,Yong Ma,Mengyao Jia,Lishun Shen,Zhenheng Zhang,Yuxin Peng,Xian Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BadmintonGRF是一个公开的多模态羽毛球数据集，包含同步的8视角RGB视频（~120 FPS）、Kistler测力板和Vicon运动捕捉数据，用于非周期球类运动的实验室级地面反作用力（GRF）估计。数据集分为Tier 1（公开2D姿态、对齐GRF和元数据）和Tier 2（受控访问的原始RGB和C3D数据），共17,425个击球片段（156次仪器化试验），并提供预处理代码、留一法交叉验证划分和十个基准基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前非周期球类运动（如羽毛球）缺乏结合实验室级传感（如测力板）与高帧率多视角视频的公开数据集，限制了基于无标记方法在真实训练场景中进行载荷估计的研究。

Method: 使用8个同步RGB摄像头（~120 FPS）、4块Kistler测力板和Vicon运动捕捉系统采集数据，通过人工验证事件、自动化质量保证和每摄像头时间偏移（带不确定性元数据）实现多模态对齐。数据集分为Tier 1（公开2D姿态+对齐GRF+元数据）和Tier 2（受控访问的原始RGB和C3D），并提供预处理代码、留一法交叉验证划分和十个基准基线。

Result: 数据集包含17,425个击球片段（156次仪器化试验），原始多视角RGB数据超过1TB；经基准加载器筛选后保留12,867个视角特定实例和1,732个唯一击球事件。Tier 1支持从2D姿态映射到GRF的基准任务。

Conclusion: BadmintonGRF是首个公开的、结合多视角视频与验证对齐的GRF数据的羽毛球数据集，填补了非周期球类运动在实验室级传感与无标记载荷估计方面的数据空白。

Abstract: Multimodal resources for non-periodic court sports with laboratory-grade sensing remain scarce: few publicly pair instrumented ground reaction force (GRF) with high-frame-rate multi-view video, limiting markerless load estimation in realistic training settings. BadmintonGRF records eight synchronized RGB views at ~120 FPS, four Kistler force plates, and Vicon motion capture (C3D) without hardware genlock across modalities; alignment combines human-verified events, automated quality assurance, and per-camera time offsets with uncertainty metadata. Tier 1 distributes pose, time-aligned GRF, metadata, and splits under CC BY-NC 4.0, enabling the primary benchmark without raw RGB or C3D; we report a Tier 1 task that maps 2D pose to GRF. Tier 2 provides raw RGB and C3D under controlled access for studies that require appearance or full kinematics. The public release contains 17,425 impact-segment archives in the 10-subject benchmark tree (156 instrumented trials; raw multi-view RGB alone exceeds 1 TB); benchmark loader gates retain 12,867 view-specific instances and 1,732 unique impacts after multi-view deduplication. We are not aware of prior public badminton corpora that combine this sensing layout with audited video--GRF alignment for impact-centric GRF estimation. We distribute preprocessing code, leave-one-subject-out splits, ten reference baselines, and optional late fusion (one deterministic test-time pass per instance; no test-time augmentation), with a within-trial diagnostic in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [95] [PubMed-Ophtha: An open resource for training ophthalmology vision-language models on scientific literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02720)
*Verena Jasmin Hallitschke,Carsten Eickhoff,Philipp Berens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍PubMed-Ophtha，一个从PubMed Central开放获取文章中提取的包含102,023对眼科图像-标题对的分层数据集。该数据集以全分辨率从PDF中提取图片，分解为面板和单个图像，并标注了成像模态和标记状态。采用两步LLM方法将图片标题拆分为面板级子标题，在人工标注数据上平均BLEU得分为0.913。面板和图像检测模型的mAP@0.50分别达到0.909和0.892，图片提取的中位IoU为0.997。同时发布了人工标注的真实数据、训练模型及完整的数据集生成流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在眼科领域具有巨大潜力，但其发展依赖大规模、高质量的图像-文本数据集，而这类资源仍然匮乏。现有数据集通常直接从PDF提取图片而缺乏细粒度分解和标注，因此需要构建一个结构化的、带有详细面板级标注和模态信息的眼科图文数据集。

Method: 从PubMed Central的15,842篇开放获取文章中提取102,023对眼科图像-标题对。图片以全分辨率从PDF提取，并分解为面板、面板标识符和单个图像；每张图像标注成像模态（彩色眼底照相、OCT、视网膜成像或其他）和标记状态（是否存在箭头等标注标记）；使用两步LLM方法将图片标题拆分为面板级子标题；评估了面板/图像检测、图片提取和标题分割的性能。

Result: 两步LLM标题分割在人工标注数据上平均句子BLEU得分为0.913；面板检测模型mAP@0.50为0.909，图像检测模型为0.892；图片提取中位IoU达0.997。数据集包含102,023对图像-标题对。

Conclusion: PubMed-Ophtha为眼科视觉-语言模型研究提供了大规模、高质量且细粒度标注的图像-文本数据集，性能指标优异。通过发布人工标注的真实数据、训练模型和完整流程，促进了研究的可重复性和后续发展。

Abstract: Vision-language models hold considerable promise for ophthalmology, but their development depends on large-scale, high-quality image-text datasets that remain scarce. We present PubMed-Ophtha, a hierarchical dataset of 102,023 ophthalmological image-caption pairs extracted from 15,842 open-access articles in PubMed Central. Unlike existing datasets, figures are extracted directly from article PDFs at full resolution and decomposed into their constituent panels, panel identifiers, and individual images. Each image is annotated with its imaging modality -- color fundus photography, optical coherence tomography, retinal imaging, or other -- and a mark status indicating the presence of annotation marks such as arrows. Figure captions are split into panel-level subcaptions using a two-step LLM approach, achieving a mean average sentence BLEU score of 0.913 on human-annotated data. Panel and image detection models reach a mAP@0.50 of 0.909 and 0.892, respectively, and figure extraction achieves a median IoU of 0.997. To support reproducibility, we additionally release the human-annotated ground-truth data, all trained models, and the full dataset generation pipeline.

</details>


### [96] [Chart-FR1: Visual Focus-Driven Fine-Grained Reasoning on Dense Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01882)
*Hongkun Pan,Yuwei Wu,Wanyi Hong,Shenghui Hu,Qitong Yan,Yi Yang,Rufei Han,Changju Zhou,Minfeng Zhu,Dongming Han,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Chart-FR1模型，通过Focus-CoT（视觉聚焦思维链）、Focus-GRPO（聚焦驱动强化学习）和HID-Chart基准数据集，解决多模态大模型在高信息密度图表上的细粒度感知、聚焦效率和自适应深度推理三大挑战，在多个图表基准上超越现有最先进模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在处理包含多子图、图例和密集标注的高信息密度（HID）图表时存在三大问题：（1）细粒度感知不足，遗漏关键视觉线索；（2）冗余或噪声视觉信息损害多模态推理性能；（3）缺乏根据视觉信息量自适应调整推理深度的能力。

Method: （1）提出Focus-CoT（视觉聚焦思维链），将推理步骤显式链接到关键视觉线索（如图像局部区域和OCR信号），增强细粒度感知；（2）提出Focus-GRPO，一种聚焦驱动的强化学习算法，包含信息效率奖励（压缩冗余视觉信息）和自适应KL惩罚机制（根据发现视觉线索的数量灵活控制推理深度）；（3）构建HID-Chart基准数据集，引入信息密度指标评估细粒度图表推理能力。

Result: 在多个图表基准上的大量实验表明，Chart-FR1在图表理解和推理任务上优于现有最先进的多模态大语言模型。

Conclusion: Chart-FR1通过聚焦驱动的细粒度推理机制，有效解决了高信息密度图表的三项核心挑战，为复杂图表理解提供了新的技术路线，开源代码和基准数据集将推动该领域进一步发展。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown considerable potential in chart understanding and reasoning tasks. However, they still struggle with high information density (HID) charts characterized by multiple subplots, legends, and dense annotations due to three major challenges: (1) limited fine-grained perception results in the omission of critical visual cues; (2) redundant or noisy visual information undermines the performance of multimodal reasoning; (3) lack of adaptive deep reasoning relative to the amount of visual information. To tackle these challenges, we present a novel focus-driven fine-grained chart reasoning model, Chart-FR1, to improve perception, focusing efficiency, and adaptive deep reasoning on HID charts. Specifically, we propose Focus-CoT, a visual focusing chain-of-thought that enhances fine-grained perception by explicitly linking reasoning steps to key visual cues, such as local image regions and OCR signals. Building on this, we introduce Focus-GRPO, a focus-driven reinforcement learning algorithm with an information-efficiency reward that compresses redundant visual information for efficient focusing, and an adaptive KL penalty mechanism that enables flexible control over reasoning depth as more visual cues are discovered. Furthermore, to fill the gap in benchmarks for HID charts, we build HID-Chart, a challenging benchmark with an information-density metric designed to evaluate fine-grained chart reasoning capabilities. Extensive experiments on multiple chart benchmarks demonstrate that Chart-FR1 outperforms state-of-the-art MLLMs in chart understanding and reasoning. Code is available at https://github.com/phkhub/Chart-FR1.

</details>


### [97] [AFFormer: Adaptive Feature Fusion Transformer for V2X Cooperative Perception under Channel Impairments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01888)
*Xi Zhou,Tao Huang,Qing-Long Han,Rana Abbas,Mostafa Rahimi Azghadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AFFormer，一种基于Transformer的鲁棒协同感知框架，通过多智能体与时序聚合、双空间注意力、不确定性引导融合及知识蒸馏策略，在V2X通信受损条件下显著提升3D目标检测的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X协同感知方法在理想通信条件下表现良好，但实际通信中存在噪声、衰落和干扰等信道损伤，导致感知特征退化，威胁自动驾驶安全。亟需提升协同感知对通信损伤的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出AFFormer框架，包含三个核心模块：1) 多智能体与时序聚合（MATA），跨智能体和时间维度进行上下文感知融合；2) 双空间注意力（DSA），高效建模空间依赖关系；3) 不确定性引导融合（UGF），基于熵对融合特征进行精炼。此外，采用教师-学生知识蒸馏策略，利用早期协同（early collaboration）的可靠监督信号对齐融合特征。

Result: 在V2XSet和DAIR-V2X数据集上，AFFormer在理想和受损通信条件下均一致优于现有方法，展现了更强的鲁棒性，同时保持了良好的效率-精度权衡。

Conclusion: AFFormer通过Transformer架构与时序-空间-不确定性多维度建模，有效缓解了通信损伤对协同感知特征的破坏，提升了自动驾驶系统的可靠性，为V2X智能交通系统的实际部署提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Accurate 3D object detection is essential for ensuring the safety of autonomous vehicles. Cooperative perception, which leverages vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication to share perceptual data, enhances detection but is vulnerable to channel impairments, such as noise, fading, and interference. To strengthen the reliability of intelligent transportation systems, this work improves the robustness of V2X cooperative perception under communication conditions that reflect common channel impairments. This paper proposes an Adaptive Feature Fusion Transformer (AFFormer), a Transformer-based framework that mitigates the adverse effects of corrupted features by modeling temporal, inter-agent, and spatial correlations. AFFormer introduces three key modules: Multi-Agent and Temporal Aggregation for context-aware fusion across agents and over time, Dual Spatial Attention for efficient modeling of spatial dependencies, and Uncertainty-Guided Fusion for entropy-driven refinement of fused features. A teacher-student knowledge distillation strategy further enhances robustness by aligning fused features with reliable early-collaboration supervision. AFFormer is validated on the V2XSet and DAIR-V2X datasets, where it consistently outperforms existing methods under both ideal and impaired communication conditions, demonstrating improved robustness to communication-induced feature degradation while maintaining a competitive efficiency-accuracy trade-off.

</details>


### [98] [Divide and Conquer: Decoupled Representation Alignment for Multimodal World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01896)
*Junyuan Xiao,Dingkang Liang,Xin Zhou,Yixuan Ye,Tongtong Su,Guangmo Yi,Bin Xia,Qiang Lyu,Shurui Shi,Jun Huang,Jianlou Si,Wenming Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出M²-REPA，首个面向多模态视频生成的表征对齐方法，通过解耦扩散模型中间特征并与多个专家基础模型对齐，显著提升视觉质量和长期一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态世界模型虽能联合生成RGB、深度、掩码等多种模态视频，但未能充分利用已有基础模型（如预训练的视觉/深度模型）中蕴含的丰富先验知识。

Method: 1) 从扩散模型中间表征中解耦出各模态特定特征；2) 设计多模态表征对齐损失（特征与对应专家模型对齐）；3) 设计模态特定解耦正则化（促进不同模态间的互补性）；4) 联合优化以充分挖掘多个基础模型的先验。

Result: 在视觉质量和长期一致性上显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过对齐不同模态的专家先验，多模态视频生成的质量和一致性可以得到有效提升。

Abstract: Emerging multi-modal world models attempt to jointly generate videos across diverse modalities (e.g., RGB, depth, and mask), yet they fail to fully exploit the rich priors of existing foundation models. We propose $M^2$-REPA, the first representation alignment method tailored for multi-modal video generation. Our key insight is that foundation models trained on different modality spaces naturally capture distinct domain-specific priors, acting as complementary "experts." Specifically, we first decouple modality-specific features from the diffusion model's intermediate representations, then align each with its corresponding expert foundation model. To this end, we design two synergistic objectives: a multi-modal representation alignment loss that enforces feature-to-expert matching, and a modality-specific decoupling regularization that encourages complementarity across different modalities. This design enables joint optimization, fully exploiting priors from multiple foundation models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms baselines in visual quality and long-term consistency.

</details>


### [99] [Behavior-Grounded Lane Representation Learning for Multi-Task Traffic Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01901)
*Rei Tamaru,Pei Li,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoLaneRep，一个基于行为的车道表示学习框架，用于交通数字孪生。该框架联合编码静态车道几何、观测车辆轨迹和运行描述符到跨相机共享语义嵌入中，通过对比跨相机对齐、辅助角色监督和时间异常检测联合训练。实验表明在零样本跨相机匹配中达到0.004横向排名误差和1.000边缘角色F1，异常检测AUROC达0.991，并可条件化扩散生成器合成满足目标规格的车道几何，准确率87.9%。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通数字孪生系统基于静态几何表示，无法捕捉动态功能语义（如车道在复杂交通条件下的运行方式），缺乏行为感知推理能力。为此，需要一种能融合静态几何与动态行为信息的车道表示学习方法。

Method: GeoLaneRep框架包含：(1) 联合编码器，将静态车道几何、观察到的车辆轨迹和运行描述符编码到共享的跨相机语义嵌入空间；(2) 联合训练目标，包括对比跨相机对齐损失、辅助角色监督损失和时间异常检测损失；(3) 使用学到的行为嵌入条件化扩散生成器，合成满足目标运行规格的车道几何。

Result: 在16个路侧相机和132个车道上的实验：(1) 零样本跨相机匹配：横向排名误差0.004，边缘角色F1分数1.000；(2) 窗口级异常检测AUROC达0.991；(3) 车道几何合成：在38个车道组上总体规格准确率87.9%。

Conclusion: GeoLaneRep为路侧观测与下游数字孪生任务提供了语义接口，支持跨相机迁移、行为感知监控和目标导向的车道合成，有效弥合了静态几何表示与动态行为语义之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Traffic digital twins are powerful tools for advanced traffic management, and most systems are built on static geometric representations. However, these representations fail to capture the dynamic functional semantics required for behavior-aware reasoning, such as how a lane operates under complex traffic conditions. To address this gap, we introduce GeoLaneRep, a behavior-grounded lane representation learning framework for traffic digital twins. GeoLaneRep jointly encodes static lane geometry, observed vehicle trajectories, and operational descriptors into a shared, cross-camera semantic embedding. The encoder is trained with a joint objective combining contrastive cross-camera alignment, auxiliary role supervision, and temporal anomaly detection. Across 16 roadside cameras and 132 lanes, the learned embeddings achieve a $0.004$ lateral-rank error and an edge-role F1 of $1.000$ in zero-shot cross-camera matching, and an AUROC of $0.991$ for window-level anomaly detection. We further show that the same behavioral embeddings can condition a diffusion-based generator to synthesize lane geometries that satisfy targeted operational specifications, with $87.9\%$ overall specification accuracy across 38 lane groups. GeoLaneRep thus provides a semantic interface between roadside observations and downstream digital twin tasks, supporting cross-camera transfer, behavior-aware monitoring, and goal-directed lane synthesis. The framework is openly available at https://github.com/raynbowy23/GeoLaneRep.

</details>


### [100] [SurgCheck: Do Vision-Language Models Really Look at Images in Surgical VQA?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01911)
*Jongmin Shin,Ka Young Kim,Eunki Cho,Seong Tae Kim,Namkee Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SurgCheck基准，通过配对问题设计（含实体名的原始问题 vs 去偏问题）量化手术视觉问答中模型对语言捷径的依赖。实验发现五个VLM在去偏问题上性能显著下降，文本消融表明动作和目标预测主要由语言捷径驱动而非视觉推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术VQA数据集存在语言捷径（问题措辞隐含限制答案空间），使模型报告性能可能反映的是语言捷径依赖而非真实视觉理解，缺乏诊断性评估工具来区分两者。

Method: 构建SurgCheck诊断基准，采用配对问题设计：每帧手术图像关联（1）含实体名的原始问题和（2）去除名称但保持视觉内容和正确答案一致的去偏问题。去偏问题通过边界框、箭头、空间位置、迂回描述四种线索保持定义明确性。评估5个VLM在零样本和微调设置下的表现，并使用LLM-as-a-judge协议评估开放域零样本回答。

Result: 五个VLM在去偏问题上均出现一致性能下降（尽管视觉输入相同）；文本消融显示动作和目标预测性能下降极小，表明这两类预测主要依赖语言捷径而非视觉推理。

Conclusion: SurgCheck提供了受控的诊断框架，揭示了被语言偏差掩盖的失败模式；强基准性能并不等同于真实视觉理解，手术VQA需要偏差感知的评估方法。

Abstract: Purpose: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown promising performance in surgical visual question answering (VQA). However, existing surgical VQA datasets often contain linguistic shortcuts, where question phrasing implicitly constrains the answer space. It remains unclear whether reported performance reflects visual understanding or reliance on such linguistic shortcuts. Methods: We introduce SurgCheck, a diagnostic benchmark for quantifying linguistic shortcut reliance in surgical VQA. SurgCheck employs a paired-question design in which each surgical frame is associated with an original question containing entity names and a less-biased counterpart that removes these names while preserving identical visual content and ground-truth answers. The resulting performance gap provides a diagnostic signal of shortcut reliance. To ensure that the less-biased question remains well-defined even without entity names, four grounding cues are incorporated: bounding box, arrow, spatial position, and periphrasis. We evaluate both general-purpose and surgical-specific VLMs under zero-shot and fine-tuned settings on SurgCheck. To evaluate open-ended zero-shot responses, we introduce an LLM-as-a-judge evaluation protocol. Results: Using SurgCheck, we observe consistent performance degradation on less-biased questions across five VLMs, despite identical visual inputs. Text-only ablation reveals minimal performance drops for action and target prediction, indicating that action and target prediction is largely driven by linguistic shortcuts rather than visual reasoning. Conclusion: SurgCheck provides a controlled diagnostic framework that exposes failure modes masked by linguistic bias in existing surgical VQA benchmarks. Our findings demonstrate that strong benchmark performance does not necessarily imply faithful visual understanding, underscoring the need for bias-aware evaluation in surgical VQA.

</details>


### [101] [EAPFusion: Intrinsic Evolving Auxiliary Prior Guidance for Infrared and Visible Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01916)
*Zhenyu Sun,Luobin Zhang,Axi Niu,Haishen Wang,Qingsen Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EAPFusion，通过自进化内在先验和动态卷积实现红外-可见光图像融合，无需外部辅助模型，在多个数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外-可见光融合方法依赖静态训练权重，无法在推理时适应场景特定内容，且注入粗粒度辅助语义时存在粒度不匹配问题，难以同时突出目标和保留细节。

Method: 提出EAPFusion，维护一组紧凑的内在先验并在不同尺度上逐步更新；利用进化后的先验动态生成卷积核，实现实例自适应的动态卷积；设计通道级融合模块，通过交错红外和可见光通道进行局部通道混合以增强跨模态互补性。

Result: 在多个数据集上（包括跨数据集评估和语义分割任务）取得了量化与定性融合结果的SOTA，并一致提升了下游任务性能。

Conclusion: EAPFusion通过自进化内在先验和动态卷积有效解决了静态权重和粒度不匹配问题，为红外-可见光融合提供了新的自适应范式。

Abstract: Infrared-visible image fusion aims to create an information-rich fused image by integrating the complementary thermal saliency from infrared sensing and fine textures from visible imaging. Such accurate fusion is essential for real-world perception applications in complex scenes, including nighttime autonomous driving, search and rescue, and surveillance, and can further benefit downstream tasks such as semantic segmentation. However, most existing fusion methods rely upon static trained weights that cannot adapt to scene-specific content at inference time, and often suffer from a granularity mismatch when coarse auxiliary semantics are injected, which makes it difficult to simultaneously highlight targets and preserve details. In this work, we propose EAPFusion to address these issues by using self-evolving intrinsic priors instead of relying on external auxiliary models. Concretely, EAPFusion maintains a compact set of intrinsic priors and progressively updates them across scales. These evolved priors are utilized to dynamically generate convolutional kernels, shifting the paradigm from fixed, pre-trained filters to instance-adaptive parameters via prior-conditioned dynamic convolution. Furthermore, we design a channel-level fusion module that shuffles and interleaves infrared and visible channels, applying local channel mixing to boost cross-modal complementarity. Experiments on different datasets, including cross-dataset evaluation and semantic segmentation, show that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art quantitative and qualitative fusion results, and consistently boosts downstream performance. Code is coming soon.

</details>


### [102] [SimPB++: Simultaneously Detecting 2D and 3D Objects from Multiple Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01924)
*Yingqi Tang,Zhaotie Meng,Erkang Cheng,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimPB++是一个端到端的多摄像头感知模型，能够同时进行2D透视图目标检测和3D鸟瞰图目标检测，通过混合解码器架构和循环3D-2D-3D优化机制实现双任务协同，在nuScenes和Argoverse2数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两阶段pipeline仅将2D检测结果作为3D检测的一次性线索，缺乏2D与3D任务的深度交互，导致多摄像头自动驾驶中2D透视图与3D鸟瞰图目标的同步感知效果不佳。

Method: 提出SimPB++，采用混合解码器架构，包含两个核心模块：动态查询分配（Dynamic Query Allocation）自适应地将2D查询分配给3D候选，自适应查询聚合（Adaptive Query Aggregation）利用多视图2D特征优化3D表示，形成循环3D-2D-3D精化。多视图2D检测使用查询组注意力（Query-group Attention）进行组内通信，并设计了Crop-and-Scale策略用于远距离感知，Propagating Denoising策略配合辅助RoI检测器。支持2D-only和全标注数据的混合监督训练。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，2D和3D检测任务均达到SOTA性能；在Argoverse2数据集上，远距离检测能力可达150m。

Conclusion: SimPB++通过端到端的混合解码器架构和循环交互机制，有效统一了多摄像头场景下的2D与3D同步检测任务，降低了对昂贵3D标签的依赖，验证了双任务深度交互的有效性。

Abstract: Simultaneous perception of 2D objects in perspective view and 3D objects in Bird's Eye View (BEV) is challenging for multi-camera autonomous driving. Existing two-stage pipelines use 2D results only as a one-time cue for 3D detection. We propose SimPB++, which simultaneously detects 2D objects in perspective and 3D objects in BEV from multiple cameras. It unifies both tasks into an end-to-end model with a hybrid decoder architecture, coupling multi-view 2D and 3D decoders interactively. Two novel modules enable deep interaction: Dynamic Query Allocation adaptively assigns 2D queries to 3D candidates, and Adaptive Query Aggregation refines 3D representations using multi-view 2D features, forming a cyclic 3D-2D-3D refinement. For multi-view 2D detection, we use Query-group Attention for intra-group communication. We also design a Crop-and-Scale strategy for long-range perception and a Propagating Denoising strategy with an auxiliary RoI detector. SimPB++ supports mixed supervision with 2D-only and fully annotated data, reducing reliance on expensive 3D labels. Experiments show state-of-the-art performance on nuScenes for both tasks and strong long-range detection (up to 150m) on Argoverse2.

</details>


### [103] [CADFS: A Big CAD Program Dataset and Framework for Computer-Aided Design with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01925)
*Vladislav Pyatov,Gleb Bobrovskikh,Saveliy Galochkin,Nikita Boldyrev,Oleg Voynov,Alexander Filippov,Gonzalo Ferrer,Peter Wonka,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了CADFS框架，通过FeatureScript表示和45万真实CAD模型数据集，使视觉语言模型能生成复杂的CAD设计历史，在文本条件CAD生成和图像重建任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式CAD系统受限于简化表示（如草图-拉伸）和有限数据集，无法生成复杂、多样化的CAD设计历史。

Method: 1) 采用FeatureScript表示来编码CAD设计历史；2) 构建包含45万真实CAD模型、覆盖15种建模操作的数据集；3) 设计新的重建管线以提取可执行的FeatureScript程序并提供多模态标注；4) 在上述表示和数据集上微调视觉语言模型。

Result: 在文本条件CAD生成和基于图像的重建任务上取得SOTA结果，生成的模型比现有框架更准确、多样且特征丰富。消融实验表明FeatureScript表示、扩展操作集和表示对齐文本描述三个组件均显著提升性能。

Conclusion: CADFS框架显著拓宽了生成式CAD的复杂度和真实感，为数据驱动的CAD生成建立了新基准。

Abstract: We introduce CADFS, a data-centric framework that enables large vision-language models to generate complex CAD design histories. Existing generative CAD systems are restricted to sketch-extrude operations due to simplified representations and limited datasets. We address this by introducing a FeatureScript-based representation and constructing a dataset of 450k real-world CAD models spanning 15 modeling operations. We obtain the dataset via a new pipeline that reconstructs clean, executable FeatureScript programs and provides multimodal annotations. Fine-tuning a VLM on this representation yields state-of-the-art results in text-conditioned CAD generation and image-based reconstruction, producing more accurate, diverse, and feature-rich designs than prior frameworks. Ablations show that each individual component of our framework, i.e., the FeatureScript representation, the extended operation set, and representation-aligned textual descriptions, significantly improves performance. Our framework substantially broadens the complexity and realism achievable in generative CAD. The CADFS framework and the new dataset are available at https://voyleg.github.io/cadfs/.

</details>


### [104] [Exploring Data-Free LoRA Transferability for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01929)
*Yuchen Wang,Wenliang Zhong,Lichen Bai,Zikai Zhou,Shitong Shao,Bojun Cheng,Shuo Chen,Shuo Yang,Zeke Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视频扩散模型中的LoRA与蒸馏变体存在权重空间不兼容问题，并揭示了其根本原因是共享奇异子空间内的频谱干扰。作者提出CASA（Cluster-Aware Spectral Arbitration），一个无数据的动态仲裁框架，通过频谱密度来平衡目标流形保护与LoRA对齐恢复，有效减轻伪影并恢复LoRA功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LoRA无法直接应用于步蒸馏或因果蒸馏等视频扩散模型变体，直接使用会导致风格退化与结构坍塌。尽管现象明显，但背后的机制尚不清晰，亟需深入理解不兼容的根本原因并设计相应解决方案。

Method: 首先从权重空间出发，分析得出不兼容源于共享奇异子空间内的频谱干扰——两种范式的路由路径存在冲突（构造性过载或破坏性抵消）。基于此，提出CASA，一个无数据框架，根据频谱密度动态仲裁目标流形保护与LoRA对齐恢复之间的平衡。

Result: 大量实验表明CASA能有效减轻伪影并恢复LoRA的功能性。

Conclusion: CASA成功解决了视频扩散模型中LoRA与蒸馏变体间的权重空间不兼容问题，为后续模型适配提供了有效且无需训练数据的解决方案。

Abstract: Video diffusion models leveraging step distillation or causal distillation have achieved remarkable performance. However, adapting existing LoRAs to these variants remains a critical challenge due to weight space mismatches. We observe that direct application leads to style degradation and structural collapse, yet the underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. To fill this gap, we delve into the weight space and identify that the incompatibility stems from spectral interference within shared functional clusters defined over singular subspaces. Specifically, our analysis reveals that while both paradigms respect spectral rigidity, they establish conflicting routing pathways that clash through constructive overload or destructive cancellation. To address this issue, we propose Cluster-Aware Spectral Arbitration (CASA), a data-free framework that dynamically arbitrates between safeguarding the target's manifold and restoring LoRA alignment based on spectral density. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CASA effectively mitigates artifacts and revives LoRA functionality. Our code is available at https://github.com/Noahwangyuchen/CASA

</details>


### [105] [ProtoFair: Fair Self-Supervised Contrastive Learning via Pseudo-Counterfactual Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01971)
*Marah Halawa,Olaf Hellwich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProtoFair是一种公平性感知对比损失方法，通过无监督原型聚类构建伪反事实对，在不修改现有SSL目标函数的情况下，将跨敏感群体的内容匹配样本在嵌入空间中拉近，从而学习对敏感属性不变的视觉表征，在CelebA和UTKFace上实现了公平性提升且保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法学习的高质量视觉表征常包含训练数据中的人口统计学偏见，而现有的公平性方法需重新设计自监督目标本身，难以适配快速演进的SSL框架。

Method: ProtoFair利用无监督原型聚类识别伪反事实对（相同聚类但不同敏感组的样本），通过对比损失将这些内容匹配的跨组样本在嵌入空间中拉近，鼓励编码器学习对敏感属性不变的表示。该方法无需目标标签，仅需敏感属性标注，可与SimCLR和SupCon无缝集成。

Result: 在CelebA和UTKFace数据集上，ProtoFair在保持竞争性精度的同时，持续提升了公平性指标。

Conclusion: ProtoFair作为一种插件式的公平性损失，能够在不修改现有SSL目标的前提下有效缓解表征中的 demographic bias，具有良好的可移植性和实用性。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning methods learn high-quality visual representations, yet recent studies show that these representations often capture demographic biases present in the training data. Existing fairness-aware methods address this by redesigning the self-supervised objective itself, limiting portability across the rapidly evolving landscape of self-supervised learning (SSL) frameworks. We propose ProtoFair, a fairness-aware contrastive loss designed to work alongside existing SSL objectives without modifying them. ProtoFair leverages unsupervised prototype clustering to identify pseudo-counterfactual pairs: samples sharing the same cluster assignment but belonging to different sensitive groups. By pulling these content-matched, cross-group samples together in the embedding space, ProtoFair encourages the encoder to learn representations that are invariant to the sensitive attribute. The method requires only sensitive attribute annotations, no target labels, and integrates seamlessly with both SimCLR and SupCon. Experiments on CelebA and UTKFace demonstrate consistent fairness improvements while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [106] [From Concept to Capability: Evaluating 3D Gaussian Splatting for Synthetic Scene Editing in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01995)
*Ali Nouri,Yifei Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Tayssir Bouraffa,Zhennan Fei,Zijian Han,Håkan Sivencrona,Anders Heyden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个系统框架，用于分析3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）在自动驾驶安全相关场景重建中的能力与局限，重点评估车辆和行人的重建保真度，为工业级ADS开发与测试提供洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统（ADS）的安全性依赖于全面且多样的数据集，但实地数据采集在覆盖罕见但可能的安全相关场景方面存在不足。3DGS虽在场景重建与编辑上展现出潜力，但其在安全关键系统中的工业级保真度评估尚未被充分探索，尤其是在动态、非受控环境、有限视角及部分遮挡条件下。

Method: 提出并实现了一个系统框架（如图1所示），专门针对车辆和行人这两类对ADS最关键的物体，从多个新颖视角（横向和纵向）系统分析3DGS在安全相关场景重建中的能力与局限性。

Result: 揭示了3DGS在多个新颖视角下重建保真度的退化情况，提供了横向和纵向两个维度上的工业级洞见。

Conclusion: 该框架能够将3DGS方法集成到真实世界的工业级ADS软件开发与测试管线中，为安全相关场景的重建质量评估提供了系统性的分析方法。

Abstract: The perception of an Autonomous Driving System (ADS) critically depends on relevant, comprehensive, and diverse datasets to ensure its safety while operating in the environment. Field data collection lacks completeness with respect to the list of rare but still possible safety-related scenarios needed for the development, verification, and validation of the ADS. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has shown promising capabilities for the reconstruction and editing of scenes based on data collected by cameras and LiDAR sensors. However, the industrial fidelity evaluation of reconstructions is underexplored, which is crucial when employing such methods in safety-related systems, especially for ADS. This becomes more challenging as ADS operates in a dynamic, uncontrolled environment with limited viewpoints and often partially occluded objects. This paper addresses this gap by proposing and implementing a framework (Fig. 1) to systematically analyze the capabilities and limitations of 3DGS for use in the reconstruction of safety-related scenes. It focuses on the quality of reconstruction for vehicles and pedestrians, which are the two most critical object classes for ADS. Our findings provide industry insights into the fidelity degradation of reconstructions from multiple novel viewpoints, both lateral and longitudinal, enabling the integration of these methods into real-world industrial AD software development and testing pipelines.

</details>


### [107] [Cross-Language Learning within Arabic Script for Low-Resource HTR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02089)
*Sana Al-azzawi,Elisa Barney,Marcus Liwicki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在低资源场景下，通过跨阿拉伯语系脚本（阿拉伯语、乌尔都语、波斯语）联合训练来提升手写文本识别性能。实验表明，联合训练在低资源设置下取得了新的最佳结果，但性能提升主要来源于脚本间共享字符，而非语言特有字符，揭示了跨脚本迁移的内在机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语系手写文本识别在标注数据稀缺时性能严重下降，而这类语言共享大量字符，因此探索跨脚本联合训练作为缓解数据不足的策略。

Method: 采用CRNN模型，在多个阿拉伯语系数据集（阿拉伯语KHATT、乌尔都语NUST-UHWR/UNHD、波斯语PHTD）上进行联合训练，并在低资源设置（100/500/1000条标注行）下进行受控的行级实验。通过统计字符级分析，区分共享字符与语言特有字符的迁移效果。

Result: 波斯语PHTD上联合训练达到9.99的CER，超越先前最佳结果；乌尔都语UNHD上CER从17.20降至14.45。字符级分析表明，性能提升主要来自脚本间共享字符，语言特有字符呈现有限或负向迁移。

Conclusion: 跨脚本联合训练能有效提升低资源阿拉伯语系HTR性能，其迁移动力主要源于脚本层面的字符重叠，而非全局均匀提升。该发现为低资源脚本家族的迁移学习提供了重要洞见。

Abstract: Handwritten Text Recognition (HTR) under limited labeled data remains a challenging problem, particularly for Arabic-script languages. Although modern sequence-based recognizers perform well in high-resource settings, their accuracy degrades sharply as training data becomes scarce. Arabic-script languages share a common writing system with substantial character overlap, motivating cross-script training as a strategy to mitigate data scarcity. We performed experiments on Arabic, Urdu, and Persian scripts and achieved improvements over single-script baselines (new SotA especially for low-resource settings). A key finding of our experiments is that cross-script transfer is largely driven by script-level overlap rather than uniform accuracy improvements. Through a statistical character-level analysis we show that gains are structurally concentrated on characters shared across scripts, while language-specific characters exhibit limited or negative transfer. These findings provide insight into transfer dynamics in low-resource script families. Detailed results include: We conduct a controlled line-level study of cross-script joint training for Arabic-script HTR under low-resource regimes (number of samples K \in 100, 500, 1000 labeled lines) on Arabic (KHATT), Urdu (NUST-UHWR), and Persian (PHTD). A CRNN model is trained on the union of multiple related Arabic-script datasets and evaluated on individual target languages. On Persian (PHTD), joint training achieves a Character Error Rate (CER) of 9.99, surpassing previously reported results despite not using the full available training data. On an Urdu dataset (UNHD), joint training reduces CER from 17.20 to 14.45. Code and data splits are released to ensure reproducibility.1

</details>


### [108] [SignMAE: Segmentation-Driven Self-Supervised Learning for Sign Language Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02094)
*Kunyuan Xie,Zhixi Cai,Kalin Stefanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于分割掩码的自监督预训练方法，通过对手部等关键身体部位进行分段掩码与重建，提升手语识别中细粒度动作表征学习能力，在WLASL、NMFs-CSL和Slovo数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语识别方法依赖在通用动作数据集上预训练的编码器，难以捕捉手部细微动作差异这一细粒度线索，因此需要一种专门针对手语识别的自监督预训练方法。

Method: 提出基于分割掩码（segmentation-based masking）的自监督预训练方法，根据关键身体部位的存在与运动状态自适应地生成掩码，通过掩码-重建目标（mask-and-reconstruct objective）进行学习，而非将手部姿态视为静态视觉token。

Result: 在WLASL、NMFs-CSL和Slovo三个手语识别基准上取得SOTA结果，在per-instance和per-class Top-1准确率上均有提升，且使用的输入帧数和模态少于对比的编码器。

Conclusion: 基于身体部位分割的掩码策略能有效引导模型关注细粒度手部动作，自监督预训练在手语识别任务上优于通用动作数据集预训练方法，且数据效率更高。

Abstract: Subtle hand differences make sign language recognition challenging, yet many existing methods rely on encoders pretrained on generic action datasets that poorly capture such fine-grained cues.
